### Lane rules (avoid collisions)
- **Finishes / bases / patterns / spec definitions + `paint-booth-0-finish-data.js` catalog data &rarr; Codex's lane.** Do not edit rendered output or finish definitions unless the **owner explicitly tells you to**.
- **Infra / tests / server / scripts / docs / CSS &rarr; fair game**, but claim the file on this board first and respect the 3-copy rule.
- If you must touch a server-side file, **edit the root copy then run `node scripts/sync-runtime-copies.js --write`** to propagate to the 2 mirrors. Never hand-edit mirror copies.
